# Tópico 103 - Comandos GNU e Unix (Peso 4)

> ⚠️ **IMPORTANTE**: Este é o tópico com MAIOR PESO na prova LPIC-101!

## 103.1 - Trabalhar na Linha de Comando

### 🐚 Shell - Conceitos Fundamentais

**O que é Shell?**
- Interpretador de comandos entre usuário e kernel
- Analisa comandos digitados e executa se válidos
- Interface de interação com o sistema

**Hierarquia do Sistema:**

![[Pasted image 20260113142913.png]]

### 📝 Shells Disponíveis


```
aaa
```


**Listar shells instalados:**

```
chsh -l ou chsh
# ou
cat /etc/shells
ubuntu@ubuntu:~$ cat /etc/shells
# /etc/shells: valid login shells
/bin/sh
/usr/bin/sh
/bin/bash
/usr/bin/bash
/bin/rbash
/usr/bin/rbash
/usr/bin/dash
/usr/bin/screen
/usr/bin/tmux
ubuntu@ubuntu:~$
```

**Bash Shell (Bourne Again Shell)**
- Desenvolvido por Stephen Bourne
- Compatível com sh
- Recursos avançados (ksh + csh)
- Compatível com POSIX

### 🔍 Tipos de Comandos

**Verificar tipo de comando:**

```
ubuntu@ubuntu:~$ type bash
bash is /usr/bin/bash
ubuntu@ubuntu:~$
ubuntu@ubuntu:~$ type cd
cd is a shell builtin
ubuntu@ubuntu:~$
ubuntu@ubuntu:~$ type ls
ls is aliased to `ls --color=auto'
ubuntu@ubuntu:~$
```


**Comandos Internos (Builtins):**
- Fazem parte do bash
- Carregados na memória
- Executados antes de comandos externos
- Exemplos: `cd`, `echo`, `export`, `set`

**Comandos Externos:**
- Programas armazenados no HD
- Precisam estar no PATH ou caminho absoluto
- Exemplos: `ls`, `grep`, `find`

**💡 Dica LPI:** Aliases são temporários (sessão atual). Para permanentes, adicione em `~/.bashrc`

> **Arquivo .bashrc**

#### 🎯 O que é?

Script de configuração do **Bash** executado automaticamente em **sessões interativas não-login**.

**Localização:** `~/.bashrc` (arquivo oculto no diretório home)

---
#### ⚙️ Quando é Executado?

✅ **SIM - Executa:**
- Ao abrir um novo terminal (Ctrl+Alt+T)
- Ao iniciar shell interativo
- Ao executar `bash` dentro de uma sessão

❌ **NÃO - Não executa:**
- Login completo no sistema (usa `~/.bash_profile` ou `~/.profile`)
- Scripts não-interativos
- Comandos via SSH sem terminal

---
#### 📋 Para que Serve?

### 1️⃣ **Aliases (Atalhos)**
```bash
alias ll='ls -lah --color=auto'
alias update='sudo apt update && sudo apt upgrade -y'
alias limpar='clear'
alias ..='cd ..'
```

##### 3️⃣ **Prompt Personalizado (PS1)**

---

#### 🔄 Diferença entre Arquivos de Configuração

| Arquivo | Quando Executa | Uso |
|---------|----------------|-----|
| `~/.bashrc` | Shell interativo não-login | Aliases, funções, prompt |
| `~/.bash_profile` | Login completo | Variáveis de ambiente, PATH |
| `~/.profile` | Login (se não houver bash_profile) | Configurações gerais |
| `/etc/bash.bashrc` | Global (todos os usuários) | Configurações do sistema |

**Ordem de execução no login:**
1. `/etc/profile`
2. `~/.bash_profile` (ou `~/.profile`)
3. `~/.bashrc` (chamado pelo bash_profile)

---

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Recarregar configurações</span><span>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># SEMPRE faça backup antes de editar!</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> ~/.bashrc ~/.bashrc.backup
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Restaurar backup</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> ~/.bashrc.backup ~/.bashrc
</span></code></pre></div>

## 📦 Estrutura Típica do .bashrc

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-w92a4awb0" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ~/.bashrc</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># 1. VERIFICAR SE É INTERATIVO</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">case</span><span> $- </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">in</span><span>
</span><span>    *i*</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">)</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span>
</span><span>      *</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">)</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">return</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">esac</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># 2. HISTÓRICO</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token assign-left environment" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">HISTCONTROL</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>ignoreboth        </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Ignora duplicatas e espaços</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token assign-left environment" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">HISTSIZE</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">10000</span><span>                </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Comandos no buffer</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token assign-left environment" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">HISTFILESIZE</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">20000</span><span>            </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Linhas no arquivo</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">shopt</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-s</span><span> histappend           </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Anexar ao histórico</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># 3. OPÇÕES DO SHELL</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">shopt</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-s</span><span> checkwinsize         </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Atualizar LINES e COLUMNS</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">shopt</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-s</span><span> cdspell              </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Corrigir erros em cd</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># 4. PROMPT</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token assign-left environment" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">PS1</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;\u@\h:\w\$ &#x27;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># 5. ALIASES</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">alias</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">ll</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;ls -lah --color=auto&#x27;</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">alias</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">la</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;ls -A&#x27;</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">alias</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">grep</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;grep --color=auto&#x27;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># 6. VARIÁVEIS DE AMBIENTE</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">export</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">EDITOR</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>vim
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">export</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left environment" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">PATH</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;</span><span class="token environment" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">$HOME</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">/bin:</span><span class="token environment" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">$PATH</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># 7. FUNÇÕES PERSONALIZADAS</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token function-name" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mkcd</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">(</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">)</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span>
</span><span>    </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mkdir</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-p</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">$1</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&amp;&amp;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">cd</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">$1</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># 8. CARREGAR CONFIGURAÇÕES EXTERNAS</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ============================================</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">if</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">[</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-f</span><span> ~/.bash_aliases </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">]</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">then</span><span>
</span><span>    </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">.</span><span> ~/.bash_aliases
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">fi</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

## ⚡ Execução Sequencial de Comandos

### Separador ";" - Execução Incondicional

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-wrjszkeoc" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Executa todos os comandos, independente de sucesso/falha</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ls</span><span> /tmp </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">df</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-hT</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mount</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

**Exemplo para dar titulo ao comando

```
echo "=== Comando 1 ===" ; ls /tmp ; echo "=== Comando 2 ===" ; df -hT
```

### Operador "&&" - AND Lógico

</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">grep</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-i</span><span> ext4 /boot/config-</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">$(</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">uname</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-r</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">)</span><span>
</span>

</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> /etc/fstab /etc/fstab.backup-</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">$(</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">date</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> +%Y%m%d</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">)</span><span>

</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Mostrar espaço do diretório home</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">du</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-sh</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">$(</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token environment" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">$HOME</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">)</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

**💡 Dica LPI:** A substituição ocorre ANTES da execução do comando principal

---

## 📜 Histórico de Comandos

### Comando history


```
# Exibir todo histórico
history

# Limpar histórico
history -c

# Append (anexar) comandos atuais ao arquivo
history -a 

# Write (sobrescrever) arquivo com buffer atual
history -w

# Evitar salvar comandos sensíveis
export HISTCONTROL=ignorespace

# Comandos que começam com ESPAÇO não são salvos
 senha_secreta="123456"   # Note o espaço antes
```

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  VOCÊ DIGITA COMANDOS                   │
│  ↓                                      │
│  BUFFER EM MEMÓRIA (history)            │
│  - Comandos da sessão atual             │
│  - Variável $HISTSIZE                   │
│  ↓                                      │
│  AO FECHAR TERMINAL                     │
│  ↓                                      │
│  ARQUIVO ~/.bash_history                │
│  - Comandos salvos no disco             │
│  - Variável $HISTFILESIZE               │
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
```
### Navegação no Histórico

**Teclas de atalho:**
- `↑` - Comando anterior
- `↓` - Comando posterior

**Executar do histórico:**

## 🌍 Variáveis em Shell

### Tipos de Variáveis

**Variável Local:**
- Visível apenas na sessão atual
- Não exportada para subshells

- Visível na sessão atual E subshells
- Exportada com `export`

### Padrão de Nomes para Variáveis no Bash

> **🎯 Regras Obrigatórias

**✅ Permitido:
- Letras (a-z, A-Z)
- Números (0-9) - **NÃO no início**
- Underscore (\_)

**❌ Proibido:
- Começar com número
- Espaços
- Caracteres especiais (!, @, #, $, %, etc)
- Hífen (-)

---

#### 📋 Convenções de Nomenclatura

**1. Case Sensitivity (Diferencia maiúsculas/minúsculas)**
```
nome=alex # Variável diferente Nome=alex # Variável diferente NOME=alex # Variável diferente
```

**2. Variáveis do Sistema (MAIÚSCULAS)**

## 💡 Boas Práticas

**✅ Nomes Descritivos**

## 📘 Comando `w` do Linux

#### 📌 Visão Geral

O comando `w` exibe informações sobre os usuários atualmente logados no sistema e o que estão fazendo, além de mostrar o uptime do sistema e a carga média (load average).

🎯 **Objetivo:** Monitorar quem está logado no sistema, suas atividades e o status geral do servidor.

---

#### 💻 Sintaxe Básica

bash

```bash
w [opções] [usuário]
```

---

#### 🔧 Opções Principais

|Opção|Descrição|
|---|---|
|`-h`|Não exibe o cabeçalho|
|`-u`|Ignora o nome de usuário ao determinar processos e tempo de CPU|
|`-s`|Formato curto (não mostra tempo de login, JCPU e PCPU)|
|`-f`|Alterna a exibição do campo FROM (de onde o usuário está conectado)|
|`-i`|Exibe endereço IP ao invés do hostname|
|`-o`|Formato antigo (compatibilidade)|

---

#### 🔧 Exemplos de Uso

##### Exibir todos os usuários logados

bash

```bash
# Exibir informações completas
w
```

**Output exemplo:**

```
 22:15:30 up 14 days,  3:42,  3 users,  load average: 0.15, 0.10, 0.08
USER     TTY      FROM             LOGIN@   IDLE   JCPU   PCPU WHAT
alex     pts/0    192.168.1.100    21:30    0.00s  0.05s  0.01s w
root     pts/1    10.0.0.50        20:15   15:00   0.10s  0.10s -bash
maria    pts/2    172.16.0.20      22:00    5:00   0.02s  0.02s vim documento.txt
```

---

##### Exibir informações de um usuário específico

bash

```bash
# Ver apenas sessões do usuário 'alex'
w alex
;
# Ver apenas sessões do root
w root
```

---

##### Formato curto (simplificado)

bash

```bash
# Exibir formato resumido
w -s
```

**Output exemplo:**

```
 22:15:30 up 14 days,  3:42,  3 users,  load average: 0.15, 0.10, 0.08
USER     TTY      FROM              IDLE WHAT
alex     pts/0    192.168.1.100     0.00s w -s
root     pts/1    10.0.0.50        15:00  -bash
maria    pts/2    172.16.0.20       5:00  vim documento.txt
```

---

##### Sem cabeçalho

bash

```bash
# Exibir apenas a lista de usuários (sem uptime e load)
w -h
```

**Output exemplo:**

```
alex     pts/0    192.168.1.100    21:30    0.00s  0.05s  0.01s w -h
root     pts/1    10.0.0.50        20:15   15:00   0.10s  0.10s -bash
maria    pts/2    172.16.0.20      22:00    5:00   0.02s  0.02s vim documento.txt
```

---

##### Exibir IP ao invés de hostname

bash

```bash
# Mostrar endereços IP
w -i
```

---

#### 📊 Entendendo a Saída

##### Linha de Cabeçalho

```
22:15:30 up 14 days, 3:42, 3 users, load average: 0.15, 0.10, 0.08
```

|Campo|Descrição|
|---|---|
|`22:15:30`|Hora atual do sistema|
|`up 14 days, 3:42`|Tempo desde o último boot (uptime)|
|`3 users`|Número de usuários logados|
|`load average: 0.15, 0.10, 0.08`|Carga média do sistema (1, 5 e 15 minutos)|

---

##### Colunas de Informação dos Usuários

|Coluna|Descrição|
|---|---|
|`USER`|Nome do usuário logado|
|`TTY`|Terminal usado (pts/0 = pseudo-terminal, tty1 = console físico)|
|`FROM`|De onde o usuário está conectado (IP ou hostname)|
|`LOGIN@`|Horário do login|
|`IDLE`|Tempo de inatividade do terminal|
|`JCPU`|Tempo de CPU usado por todos os processos anexados ao terminal|
|`PCPU`|Tempo de CPU usado pelo processo atual (campo WHAT)|
|`WHAT`|Comando/processo que o usuário está executando atualmente|

---

#### 🔍 Casos de Uso Práticos

##### Monitorar atividade suspeita

bash

```bash
# Verificar quem está logado e de onde
w -i
;
# Combinar com watch para monitoramento contínuo
watch -n 5 w
```

---

##### Identificar usuários ociosos

bash
```bash
# Ver usuários com mais de 1 hora de idle
w | awk '$5 ~ /:[0-9][0-9]/ || $5 ~ /days/'
```

---

##### Verificar carga do sistema

bash

```bash
# Apenas a linha de uptime e load average
w | head -1
```

---

##### Auditoria de segurança

bash

```bash
# Listar todos os IPs conectados
w -h | awk '{print $3}' | sort -u
;
# Ver quantas sessões cada usuário tem
w -h | awk '{print $1}' | sort | uniq -c
```

---

#### 🆚 Comandos Relacionados

|Comando|Função|Diferença|
|---|---|---|
|`w`|Usuários logados + atividade + load|Mais completo|
|`who`|Usuários logados + terminal + horário|Mais simples, sem load|
|`whoami`|Exibe apenas seu usuário atual|Muito específico|
|`users`|Lista apenas nomes de usuários|Minimalista|
|`last`|Histórico de logins|Dados históricos|
|`uptime`|Apenas uptime e load average|Sem informações de usuários|

---

## 📊 Tabela Comparativa

| Tipo | Padrão | Exemplo |
|------|--------|---------|
| **Sistema** | MAIÚSCULAS | `PATH`, `HOME`, `USER` |
| **Script local** | minúsculas_underscore | `nome_arquivo`, `contador` |
| **Constante** | MAIÚSCULAS | `MAX_TENTATIVAS`, `VERSAO` |
| **Temporária** | prefixo_tmp | `TMP_FILE`, `tmp_resultado` |
| **Configuração** | prefixo_cfg | `CFG_HOST`, `CFG_PORT` |
| **Array** | plural | `usuarios`, `arquivos` |

---

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token shebang" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">#!/bin/bash</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Variáveis de sistema</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">usuario_atual</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token environment" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">$USER</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">hostname_sistema</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">$(</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">hostname</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">)</span><span>

</span><span></span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">log_timestamp</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">$(</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">date</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S&#x27;</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">)</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Variáveis de verificação</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">uso_cpu</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">$(</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">top</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-bn1</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">grep</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;Cpu(s)&quot;</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">awk</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;{print $2}&#x27;</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">uso_memoria</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">$(</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">free</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-m</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">awk</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;NR==2{printf &quot;%.2f&quot;, $3*100/$2}&#x27;</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">uso_disco</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">$(</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">df</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-h</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> / </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">awk</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;NR==2{print $5}&#x27;</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">)</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Limites</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">readonly</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">LIMITE_CPU</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">80</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">readonly</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">LIMITE_MEMORIA</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">90</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">readonly</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">LIMITE_DISCO</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">85</span><span>

| Variável   | Descrição                               | Exemplo          |
| ---------- | --------------------------------------- | ---------------- |
| `$HOME`    | Diretório home do usuário               | `/home/alex`     |
| `$PATH`    | Diretórios de executáveis               | `/usr/bin:/bin`  |
| `$PWD`     | Diretório atual                         | `/tmp`           |
| `$OLDPWD`  | Diretório anterior                      | `/home/alex`     |
| `$USER`    | Nome do usuário                         | `alex`           |
| `$UID`     | ID do usuário                           | `1000`           |
| `$SHELL`   | Shell padrão                            | `/bin/bash`      |
| `$TERM`    | Tipo de terminal                        | `xterm-256color` |
| `$DISPLAY` | Display do X11                          | `:0`             |
| `$LOGNAME` | Nome de login                           | `alex`           |
| `$SHLVL`   | **contador de profundidade** dos shells | `1, 2, 3...`     |

**Exemplos práticos:**
<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-s3ffgj9ho" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Ir para home</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">cd</span><span> </span><span class="token environment" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">$HOME</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ou</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">cd</span><span> ~
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Voltar para diretório anterior</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">cd</span><span> </span><span class="token environment" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">$OLDPWD</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ou</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">cd</span><span> -
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Mostrar shell atual</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span> </span><span class="token environment" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">$SHELL</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Verificar se é root</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">[</span><span> </span><span class="token environment" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">$UID</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-eq</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">0</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">]</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&amp;&amp;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;Sou root&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">||</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;Não sou root&quot;</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Nome da máquina (hostname)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">uname</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-n</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Versão do kernel</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">uname</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-r</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Nome do sistema operacional</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">uname</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-s</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Arquitetura do hardware</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">uname</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-m</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Tudo de uma vez</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">uname</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-a</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

**Exemplos práticos:**

## 📚 Manuais e Documentação

### Comando man

**Seções do manual:**
1. Comandos de usuário
2. Chamadas de sistema
3. Bibliotecas
4. Dispositivos (`/dev`)
5. Arquivos de configuração
6. Jogos
7. Diversos
8. Comandos administrativos (root)
9. Rotinas do kernel

**Sintaxe:**

**Navegação no man:**
- `Espaço` - Próxima página
- `b` - Página anterior
- `/texto` - Buscar texto
- `n` - Próxima ocorrência
- `q` - Sair

### Comando info

####💻 Sintaxe Básica

bash

```bash
info [opções] [comando/tópico]
```

---

#### 🔧 Exemplos de Uso

##### Exibir informações sobre um comando

bash

```bash
# Documentação do comando 'ls'
info ls
;
# Documentação do comando 'grep'
info grep
;
# Documentação do bash
info bash
;
# Documentação do coreutils (utilitários principais)
info coreutils
```

---
##### Navegar pela documentação geral

bash

```bash
# Abrir o índice principal de documentação
info
;
# Abrir tópico específico
info gcc
;
# Abrir seção específica de um tópico
info gcc 'Invoking GCC'
```

---

#### ⌨️ Navegação no `info`

Quando você abre o `info`, pode navegar usando as seguintes teclas:

| Tecla                    | Função                                    |
| ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- |
| **Espaço**               | Avançar uma página                        |
| **Backspace** ou **Del** | Voltar uma página                         |
| **n**                    | Próximo nó (next)                         |
| **p**                    | Nó anterior (previous)                    |
| **u**                    | Subir um nível (up)                       |
| **l**                    | Voltar ao último nó visitado (last)       |
| **Tab**                  | Pular para o próximo link                 |
| **Enter**                | Seguir o link sob o cursor                |
| **d**                    | Voltar ao diretório principal (directory) |
| **t**                    | Ir para o topo do documento atual (top)   |
| **q**                    | Sair do `info` (quit)                     |
| **?**                    | Exibir ajuda de navegação                 |
| **s**                    | Buscar texto (search)                     |
| **Ctrl+s**               | Buscar para frente                        |
| **Ctrl+r**               | Buscar para trás                          |

### Comando which

#### 💻 Sintaxe Básica

bash
```bash
which [opções] <comando>
```

---

#### 🔧 Exemplos de Uso

##### Localizar executável de um comando

bash
```bash
# Encontrar localização do python
which python
# Output: /usr/bin/python
;
# Encontrar localização do bash
which bash
# Output: /bin/bash
;
# Encontrar localização do gcc
which gcc
# Output: /usr/bin/gcc
;
# Encontrar localização do vim
which vim
# Output: /usr/bin/vim
```

---

##### Localizar múltiplos executáveis

bash
```bash
# Encontrar localização de vários comandos de uma vez
which python bash gcc vim
# Output:
# /usr/bin/python
# /bin/bash
# /usr/bin/gcc
# /usr/bin/vim
```

### Diferença: `cat arquivo` vs `cat < arquivo`

| Aspecto                | `cat arquivo`               | `cat < arquivo`  |
| ---------------------- | --------------------------- | ---------------- |
| **Quem abre**          | Comando `cat`               | Shell (bash)     |
| **Entrada**            | Argumento (nome do arquivo) | stdin (conteúdo) |
| **Múltiplos arquivos** | ✅ Sim (`cat f1 f2`)         | ❌ Não            |
| **Erro de permissão**  | `cat:` erro                 | `bash:` erro     |
| **Resultado visual**   | Idêntico                    | Idêntico         |
|                        |                             |                  |

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">less</span><span> /var/log/syslog
</span></code></pre></div>

**Navegação:**
- `Setas` - Navegar
- `Espaço` / `PgDn` - Próxima página
- `b` / `PgUp` - Página anterior
- `Enter` - Próxima linha
- `/texto` - Buscar
- `n` - Próxima ocorrência
- `N` - Ocorrência anterior
- `q` - Sair

---

## Comandos head e tail

### head - Primeiras Linhas

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-kozjhwv09" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Primeiras 10 linhas (padrão)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">head</span><span> /etc/passwd
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Primeiras 20 linhas</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">head</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-n</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">20</span><span> /etc/shadow
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">head</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-20</span><span> /etc/shadow
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Tudo EXCETO últimas 20 linhas</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">head</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-n</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-20</span><span> /etc/passwd
</span></code></pre></div>

### tail - Últimas Linhas

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-g3fft0zu4" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Últimas 10 linhas (padrão)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tail</span><span> /etc/group
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Últimas 15 linhas</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tail</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-n</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">15</span><span> /etc/group
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># A partir da linha 15 até o final</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">nl</span><span> /etc/passwd </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tail</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-n</span><span> +15
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Acompanhar arquivo em tempo real (MUITO IMPORTANTE!)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tail</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-f</span><span> /var/log/syslog
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tail</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-f</span><span> /var/log/messages
</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Monitorar logs do sistema</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tail</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-f</span><span> /var/log/syslog

## Comandos Avançados de Texto

### uniq - Remover Duplicatas

**⚠️ IMPORTANTE:** Só remove linhas CONSECUTIVAS duplicadas. Use com `sort` antes!

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-5ndw3fu60" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Remover duplicatas consecutivas</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">uniq</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Mostrar apenas linhas duplicadas</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">uniq</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-d</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Mostrar apenas linhas únicas</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">uniq</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-u</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Contar ocorrências</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">uniq</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-c</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Ignorar case</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">uniq</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-i</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-e</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;debian</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">\n</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">centos</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">\n</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">debian</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">\n</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">ubuntu</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">\n</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">centos&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> /tmp/distro.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Ordenar E remover duplicatas</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sort</span><span> /tmp/distro.txt </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">uniq</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Contar ocorrências</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sort</span><span> /tmp/distro.txt </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">uniq</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-c</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

### od - Dump Octal

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-zho32s3s6" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Exibir em octal</span><span>
</span>od /etc/passwd
<!-- -->
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Exibir em hexadecimal</span><span>
</span><span>od </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-x</span><span> /etc/passwd
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Exibir caracteres</span><span>
</span><span>od </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-c</span><span> /etc/passwd
</span></code></pre></div>

### paste - Mesclar Arquivos

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-mnrfl7meb" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Mesclar lado a lado</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">paste</span><span> arquivo1.txt arquivo2.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Mesclar com delimitador personalizado</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">paste</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-d</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;,&#x27;</span><span> arquivo1.txt arquivo2.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Exemplo com arquivos do sistema</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">paste</span><span> /etc/group /etc/gshadow
</span></code></pre></div>

### split - Dividir Arquivos

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-t60wd5pi5" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Dividir por número de linhas</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">split</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-l</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">3</span><span> arquivo.txt partes_
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Dividir por tamanho</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">split</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-b</span><span> 1M arquivo.iso partes_
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Reunir novamente</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cat</span><span> partes_* </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> arquivo_reunido.txt
</span></code></pre></div>

**Exemplo prático:**
<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-ge8fkzr1w" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Criar arquivo de teste</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-e</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;slackware</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">\n</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">debian</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">\n</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">centos</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">\n</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">redhat</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">\n</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">opensuse</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">\n</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">ubuntu</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">\n</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">fedora&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> /tmp/distro.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Dividir em partes de 3 linhas</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">split</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-l</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">3</span><span> /tmp/distro.txt /tmp/distros_partes_
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Listar partes</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ls</span><span> /tmp/distros_partes_*
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Reunir</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cat</span><span> /tmp/distros_partes_* </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> /tmp/distros_copia.txt
</span></code></pre></div>
