## Comandos de Manipulação de Texto

### sed - Stream Editor

**Substituir texto:**
<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-muuu0gb93" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Substituir primeira ocorrência na linha</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sed</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;s/root/bastos/&#x27;</span><span> /etc/passwd
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Substituir TODAS ocorrências (global)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sed</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;s/root/bastos/g&#x27;</span><span> /etc/passwd
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Substituir segunda ocorrência</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sed</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;s/root/bastos/2&#x27;</span><span> /etc/passwd
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Substituir apenas na linha 1</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sed</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;1s/root/bastos/g&#x27;</span><span> /etc/passwd
</span></code></pre></div>

**Deletar linhas:**
<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-s40ji6pwx" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Deletar linhas 1 a 3</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sed</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;1,3d&#x27;</span><span> /etc/passwd
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Deletar linhas comentadas</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sed</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;/^#/d&#x27;</span><span> /etc/fstab
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Deletar linhas vazias</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sed</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;/^$/d&#x27;</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span></code></pre></div>

**Usar delimitadores alternativos:**
<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-nk8kugrhd" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Trocar / por @ (útil com caminhos)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sed</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;s|/|@|g&#x27;</span><span> /etc/fstab
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sed</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;s#/home#/usuarios#g&#x27;</span><span> /etc/passwd
</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sed</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-i</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;s/old/new/g&#x27;</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sed</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-i.bak</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;s/old/new/g&#x27;</span><span> arquivo.txt  </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Cria backup</span><span>

### tr - Translate Characters

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-wn1hr5ywq" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Minúsculas para maiúsculas</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;texto&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tr</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;a-z&#x27;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;A-Z&#x27;</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;texto&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tr</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;[:lower:]&#x27;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;[:upper:]&#x27;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Maiúsculas para minúsculas</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;TEXTO&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tr</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;A-Z&#x27;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;a-z&#x27;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Trocar espaços por underscore</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;frase com espaços&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tr</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27; &#x27;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;_&#x27;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Remover caracteres</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;texto123&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tr</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-d</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;0-9&#x27;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Comprimir caracteres repetidos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;aaaaabbbbb&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tr</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-s</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;ab&#x27;</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Converter arquivo para maiúsculas</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cat</span><span> arquivo.txt </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tr</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;a-z&#x27;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;A-Z&#x27;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> arquivo_upper.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Remover quebras de linha</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cat</span><span> arquivo.txt </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tr</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-d</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;\n&#x27;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Trocar : por espaço</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cat</span><span> /etc/passwd </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tr</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;:&#x27;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27; &#x27;</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

### cut - Extrair Colunas

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Extrair campos 1 e 3 (delimitador :)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cut</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-d</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;:&#x27;</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-f</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1,3</span><span> /etc/group
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Extrair campos com delimitador de saída personalizado</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cut</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-d</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;:&#x27;</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-f</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1,3</span><span> --output-delimiter</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;=&#x27;</span><span> /etc/group
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Extrair caracteres 1 a 10</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cut</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-c</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1</span><span>-10 /etc/passwd
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Extrair do caractere 5 em diante</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cut</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-c</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">5</span><span>- /etc/passwd
</span></code></pre></div>

### Comando ls

**Opções essenciais:**

| Opção | Descrição |
|-------|-----------|
| `-l` | Formato longo (detalhes) |
| `-a` | Mostrar ocultos |
| `-h` | Tamanhos legíveis (K, M, G) |
| `-F` | Indicador de tipo (/, *, @) |
| `-r` | Ordem reversa |
| `-R` | Recursivo |
| `-t` | Ordenar por data |
| `-S` | Ordenar por tamanho |
| `-1` | Uma coluna |
| `--color` | Saída colorida |

**Exemplos práticos:**
<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-es9fqekyc" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Listagem detalhada com tamanhos legíveis</span><span>

</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ls</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-lt</span><span>
</span>

</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ls</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-lS</span><span>
</span>

</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Com indicadores de tipo</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ls</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-F</span><span>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Cópia simples</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> origem destino
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Preservar atributos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-p</span><span> origem destino
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">--preserve</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>all origem destino
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Modo interativo (pergunta antes de sobrescrever)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-i</span><span> origem destino
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Recursivo (diretórios)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-r</span><span> /etc /backup/
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Modo arquivo (-a = -dR --preserve=all)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-a</span><span> /etc /backup/
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Verbose</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-v</span><span> origem destino
</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Backup de arquivo de configuração</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-p</span><span> /etc/fstab /etc/fstab.backup
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Copiar diretório completo</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-a</span><span> /home/alex/Documents /backup/
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Copiar múltiplos arquivos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> arquivo1.txt arquivo2.txt /tmp/
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Copiar com estrutura de diretórios</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">--parents</span><span> /etc/network/interfaces /backup/
</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Mover arquivo</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mv</span><span> origem destino
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Renomear arquivo</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mv</span><span> nome_antigo.txt nome_novo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Modo interativo</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mv</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-i</span><span> origem destino
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Verbose</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mv</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-v</span><span> origem destino
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Não sobrescrever</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mv</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-n</span><span> origem destino
</span></code></pre></div>

**Exemplos práticos:**
<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-yx2l43bvb" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Renomear</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mv</span><span> /tmp/passwd /tmp/passwd.old
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Mover para outro diretório</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mv</span><span> arquivo.txt /home/alex/Documents/
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Mover múltiplos arquivos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mv</span><span> *.txt /tmp/
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Renomear com backup</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mv</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-b</span><span> arquivo.txt arquivo.old
</span></code></pre></div>

## Gerenciamento de Diretórios

### mkdir - Criar Diretórios

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-2tn6c3nf6" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Criar diretório simples</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mkdir</span><span> novo_dir
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Criar árvore de diretórios</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mkdir</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-p</span><span> /tmp/nivel1/nivel2/nivel3
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Definir permissões na criação</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mkdir</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-m</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">750</span><span> /tmp/lpic1
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Verbose</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mkdir</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-v</span><span> /tmp/teste
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Criar múltiplos diretórios</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mkdir</span><span> dir1 dir2 dir3
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">mkdir</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-p</span><span> projeto/</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span>src,docs,tests</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

### rmdir - Remover Diretórios Vazios

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-ldpf6x5ko" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Remover diretório vazio</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">rmdir</span><span> diretorio_vazio
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Remover hierarquia vazia</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">rmdir</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-p</span><span> nivel1/nivel2/nivel3
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Verbose</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">rmdir</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-v</span><span> diretorio
</span></code></pre></div>

### rm - Remover Arquivos e Diretórios

**⚠️ CUIDADO:** Não há lixeira! Remoção é permanente!

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-jv1flezk4" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Remover arquivo</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">rm</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Modo interativo (RECOMENDADO)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">rm</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-i</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Forçar remoção</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">rm</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-f</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Remover diretório e conteúdo (PERIGOSO!)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">rm</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-r</span><span> diretorio/
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">rm</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-rf</span><span> diretorio/  </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Força remoção recursiva</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Verbose</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">rm</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-v</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Remover diretório vazio</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">rm</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-d</span><span> diretorio_vazio
</span></code></pre></div>

**🎯 Dicas de Segurança:**

</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">alias</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">rm</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&#x27;rm -i&#x27;</span><span>

</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /var/log </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-mtime</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-1</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Modificado há mais de 7 dias</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /tmp </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-mtime</span><span> +7
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Modificado há exatamente 3 dias</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /home </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-mtime</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">3</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Modificado nos últimos 60 minutos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /var/log </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-mmin</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-60</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Acessado nos últimos 30 minutos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /tmp </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-amin</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-30</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Status alterado há menos de 24h</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /etc </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-ctime</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-1</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

### Busca por Tamanho

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Maior que 100MB</span><span>

</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /tmp </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-size</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-1k</span><span>
</span>

</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> / </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-size</span><span> 50M
</span>

</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Por usuário</span><span>

</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> / </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-nogroup</span><span>
