8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Listar detalhes dos arquivos encontrados</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /etc </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.conf&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-exec</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ls</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-l</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">\</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Copiar arquivos encontrados</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /home </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.txt&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-exec</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span> /backup/ </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">\</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Deletar arquivos encontrados</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /tmp </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.tmp&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-exec</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">rm</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">\</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Executar múltiplos comandos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /var/log </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.log&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-exec</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">gzip</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">\</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Confirmar antes de executar</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /tmp </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.old&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-ok</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">rm</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">\</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Usar com xargs (mais eficiente)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /home </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.txt&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">xargs</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ls</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-l</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

**Exemplos Práticos Completos:**

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-o4vrxuyva" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Arquivos modificados hoje</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /home </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-type</span><span> f </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-mtime</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">0</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Arquivos grandes (&gt;100MB) modificados na última semana</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> / </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-type</span><span> f </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-size</span><span> +100M </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-mtime</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-7</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Arquivos .conf em /etc modificados nas últimas 24h</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /etc </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.conf&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-type</span><span> f </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-ctime</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-1</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Links em /usr/lib modificados ontem</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /usr/lib </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-type</span><span> l </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-ctime</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-1</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Arquivos sem dono (órfãos)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> / </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-nouser</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-ls</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Arquivos SUID (segurança)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> / </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-perm</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-4000</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-ls</span><span> </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">2</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span>/dev/null
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Arquivos world-writable (risco de segurança)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> / </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-perm</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-002</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-type</span><span> f </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-ls</span><span> </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">2</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span>/dev/null
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Limpar arquivos temporários antigos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /tmp </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-type</span><span> f </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-mtime</span><span> +7 </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-exec</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">rm</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">\</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Backup de arquivos de configuração</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /etc </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.conf&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-exec</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span> /backup/configs/ </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">\</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Encontrar e compactar logs antigos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /var/log </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.log&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-mtime</span><span> +30 </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-exec</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">gzip</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">\</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">;</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

---

## Compactação e Arquivamento

### gzip / gunzip

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-y52inotum" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Compactar arquivo</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">gzip</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Resultado: arquivo.txt.gz</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Descompactar</span><span>
</span>gunzip arquivo.txt.gz
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ou</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">gzip</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-d</span><span> arquivo.txt.gz
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Manter arquivo original</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">gzip</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-k</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Nível de compressão (1-9, 9=máximo)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">gzip</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-9</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Verbose</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">gzip</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-v</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Recursivo em diretório</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">gzip</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-r</span><span> diretorio/
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Ver conteúdo sem descompactar</span><span>
</span>zcat arquivo.txt.gz
<!-- -->zless arquivo.txt.gz
<span>zgrep </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;padrão&quot;</span><span> arquivo.txt.gz
</span></code></pre></div>

### bzip2 / bunzip2

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-p2s1eg0or" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Compactar (melhor compressão que gzip)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">bzip2</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Resultado: arquivo.txt.bz2</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Descompactar</span><span>
</span>bunzip2 arquivo.txt.bz2
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ou</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">bzip2</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-d</span><span> arquivo.txt.bz2
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Manter original</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">bzip2</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-k</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Verbose</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">bzip2</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-v</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Ver conteúdo</span><span>
</span>bzcat arquivo.txt.bz2
<!-- -->bzless arquivo.txt.bz2
<span>bzgrep </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;padrão&quot;</span><span> arquivo.txt.bz2
</span></code></pre></div>

### xz / unxz

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-qlusblnul" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Compactar (melhor compressão)</span><span>
</span>xz arquivo.txt
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Resultado: arquivo.txt.xz</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Descompactar</span><span>
</span>unxz arquivo.txt.xz
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ou</span><span>
</span><span>xz </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-d</span><span> arquivo.txt.xz
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Manter original</span><span>
</span><span>xz </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-k</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Nível de compressão (0-9)</span><span>
</span><span>xz </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-9</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Ver conteúdo</span><span>
</span>xzcat arquivo.txt.xz
<!-- -->xzless arquivo.txt.xz
</code></pre></div>

**Comparação de Compressão:**
- **gzip**: Rápido, compressão moderada
- **bzip2**: Médio, boa compressão
- **xz**: Lento, melhor compressão

---

## Comando tar - Arquivamento

### Criar Arquivos

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-nazob6b91" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Criar arquivo tar</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> cvf arquivo.tar /etc
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># c=create, v=verbose, f=file</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Criar e compactar com gzip</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> cvzf arquivo.tar.gz /etc
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># z=gzip</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Criar e compactar com bzip2</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> cvjf arquivo.tar.bz2 /etc
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># j=bzip2</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Criar e compactar com xz</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> cvJf arquivo.tar.xz /etc
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># J=xz (maiúsculo!)</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

### Extrair Arquivos

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-gotzqo6vn" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Extrair tar</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> xvf arquivo.tar
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Extrair para diretório específico</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> xvf arquivo.tar </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-C</span><span> /tmp
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Extrair tar.gz</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> xvzf arquivo.tar.gz
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Extrair tar.bz2</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> xvjf arquivo.tar.bz2
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Extrair tar.xz</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> xvJf arquivo.tar.xz
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Extrair arquivo específico</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> xvf arquivo.tar caminho/arquivo.txt
</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Listar conteúdo</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> tvf arquivo.tar
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Listar tar.gz</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> tvzf arquivo.tar.gz
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Listar tar.bz2</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> tvjf arquivo.tar.bz2
</span></code></pre></div>

### Opções Úteis

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-8itkufhqz" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Preservar permissões</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> cvpf arquivo.tar /etc
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Excluir arquivos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> cvf arquivo.tar </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">--exclude</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.log&quot;</span><span> /var/log
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Adicionar a arquivo existente</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> rvf arquivo.tar novo_arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Verificar integridade</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> tvf arquivo.tar
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Diferença entre tar e filesystem</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> dvf arquivo.tar
</span></code></pre></div>

**Exemplos Práticos:**

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-5r8og6gwd" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Backup completo de /etc</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> cvzf /backup/etc-</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">$(</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">date</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)"> +%Y%m%d</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">)</span><span>.tar.gz /etc
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Backup de home excluindo cache</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> cvzf home-backup.tar.gz </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">\</span><span>
</span><span>    </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">--exclude</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*/.cache&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">\</span><span>
</span><span>    </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">--exclude</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*/Downloads&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">\</span><span>
</span>    /home/alex
<!-- -->
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Extrair e preservar permissões</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> xvpzf backup.tar.gz </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-C</span><span> /restore/
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Backup incremental</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> cvzf backup-full.tar.gz /data
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tar</span><span> cvzf backup-inc.tar.gz --newer-mtime</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;2026-01-01&quot;</span><span> /data
</span></code></pre></div>

## Comandos cpio e dd

### cpio - Copy In/Out

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-c1mgw489x" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Criar arquivo (modo -o)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ls</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> cpio </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-ov</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> arquivo.cpio
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /boot </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-type</span><span> f </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> cpio </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-ov</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> boot.cpio
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Extrair arquivo (modo -i)</span><span>
</span><span>cpio </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-iv</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&lt;</span><span> arquivo.cpio
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Extrair com estrutura de diretórios (-d)</span><span>
</span><span>cpio </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-ivd</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&lt;</span><span> arquivo.cpio
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Listar conteúdo (-t)</span><span>
</span><span>cpio </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-tv</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&lt;</span><span> arquivo.cpio
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Copiar diretórios</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /origem </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-depth</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> cpio </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-pdm</span><span> /destino
</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Backup de módulos do kernel</span><span>

### dd - Disk Dump

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Copiar partição</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">dd</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">if</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/dev/sda1 </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">of</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/tmp/sda1.img
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Criar arquivo vazio</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">dd</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">if</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/dev/zero </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">of</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/tmp/file.img </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">bs</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>1M </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">count</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1024</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Backup de MBR</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">dd</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">if</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/dev/sda </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">of</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/tmp/mbr.img </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">bs</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">512</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">count</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Restaurar MBR</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">dd</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">if</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/tmp/mbr.img </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">of</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/dev/sda </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">bs</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">512</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">count</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Clonar disco</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">dd</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">if</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/dev/sda </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">of</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/dev/sdb </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">bs</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>4M </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">status</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>progress
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Criar ISO de CD/DVD</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">dd</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">if</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/dev/cdrom </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">of</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/tmp/cd.iso </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">bs</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">2048</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Apagar disco com segurança</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">dd</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">if</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/dev/urandom </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">of</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/dev/sda </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">bs</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>4M </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">status</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>progress
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Testar velocidade de disco</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">dd</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">if</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/dev/zero </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">of</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>/tmp/test </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">bs</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>1M </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">count</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1024</span><span> </span><span class="token assign-left" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">oflag</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">=</span><span>direct
</span></code></pre></div>

**Opções importantes:**
- `if=` - Input file (origem)
- `of=` - Output file (destino)
- `bs=` - Block size (tamanho do bloco)
- `count=` - Número de blocos
- `status=progress` - Mostrar progresso
- `conv=noerror` - Continuar em caso de erro

---

## 103.4 - Redirecionamentos e Pipes

### Descritores de Arquivo

| Descritor | Nome | Número | Padrão |
|-----------|------|--------|--------|
| stdin | Entrada padrão | 0 | Teclado |
| stdout | Saída padrão | 1 | Tela |
| stderr | Erro padrão | 2 | Tela |

### Redirecionamento de Saída

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Redirecionar stdout (sobrescreve)</span><span>
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">1</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Redirecionar stdout (anexa)</span><span>
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;&gt;</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">1</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;&gt;</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Redirecionar stderr</span><span>
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">2</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> erros.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Redirecionar stderr (anexa)</span><span>
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">2</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;&gt;</span><span> erros.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Redirecionar stdout E stderr</span><span>
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&amp;&gt;</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> arquivo.txt </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">2</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">&amp;1</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Redirecionar stdout E stderr (anexa)</span><span>
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;&gt;</span><span> arquivo.txt </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">2</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">&amp;1</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Descartar saída (buraco negro)</span><span>
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> /dev/null
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">2</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> /dev/null
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&amp;&gt;</span><span> /dev/null
</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Redirecionar stdin</span><span>

</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Exemplo: enviar arquivo por email</span><span>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Criar arquivo com múltiplas linhas</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cat</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> arquivo.txt </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&lt;&lt;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">EOF
