### Pipe (|)

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-1oy2t0jgt" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Conectar saída de um comando à entrada de outro</span><span>
</span><span>comando1 </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> comando2
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Exemplos práticos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ls</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-l</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">grep</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;.txt&quot;</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ps</span><span> aux </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">grep</span><span> apache
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cat</span><span> /etc/passwd </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sort</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">less</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">dmesg</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tail</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-n</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">50</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">df</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-h</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">grep</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;/dev/sd&quot;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Múltiplos pipes</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cat</span><span> /etc/passwd </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cut</span><span> -d: </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-f1</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sort</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">uniq</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">wc</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-l</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Pipe com redirecionamento</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ps</span><span> aux </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">grep</span><span> apache </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> processos.txt </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">2</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">&amp;1</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

**Exemplos Práticos Completos:**

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-1nke6m97f" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Separar saída e erros</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ls</span><span> /tmp /naoexiste </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> saida.txt </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">2</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> erros.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Tudo para mesmo arquivo</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ls</span><span> /tmp /naoexiste </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> tudo.txt </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">2</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">&amp;1</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Anexar logs</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">date</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;&gt;</span><span> /var/log/meu_script.log
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;Executando backup&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;&gt;</span><span> /var/log/meu_script.log </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">2</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">&amp;1</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Descartar erros, manter saída</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> / </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.conf&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">2</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span>/dev/null
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Descartar tudo</span><span>
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&amp;&gt;</span><span>/dev/null
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Criar arquivo vazio</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> arquivo_vazio.txt
</span></code></pre></div>

---

## Comandos tee e xargs

### tee - Dividir Saída

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-n4p33rgai" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Enviar para arquivo E tela</span><span>
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tee</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Anexar (não sobrescrever)</span><span>
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tee</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-a</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Múltiplos arquivos</span><span>
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tee</span><span> arquivo1.txt arquivo2.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Com sudo (para arquivos protegidos)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;texto&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sudo</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tee</span><span> /etc/arquivo_protegido
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Ignorar interrupções</span><span>
</span><span>comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tee</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-i</span><span> arquivo.txt
</span></code></pre></div>

**Exemplos práticos:**
<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-c1by0bfq9" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Log e visualização simultânea</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ls</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-la</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tee</span><span> listagem.txt
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Backup de configuração antes de editar</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cat</span><span> /etc/fstab </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tee</span><span> /etc/fstab.backup </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">sudo</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">vim</span><span> -
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Pipeline com log</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ps</span><span> aux </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">grep</span><span> apache </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tee</span><span> processos.log </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">wc</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-l</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Append em log</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">date</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">tee</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-a</span><span> /var/log/execucoes.log
</span></code></pre></div>

### xargs - Construir Comandos

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-mg5d6i0cl" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Passar argumentos para comando</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">echo</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;arquivo.txt&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">xargs</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cat</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Executar comando para cada linha</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cat</span><span> lista.txt </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">xargs</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-I</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span> comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Limitar argumentos por execução</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">.</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.txt&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">xargs</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-n</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ls</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-l</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Executar em paralelo</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">.</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.jpg&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">xargs</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-P</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">4</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-I</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span> convert </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span>.png
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Perguntar antes de executar</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">.</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.tmp&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">xargs</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-p</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">rm</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Lidar com espaços em nomes</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">.</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.txt&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-print0</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">xargs</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-0</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ls</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-l</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Buscar e listar detalhes</span><span>

</span>

</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> /var/log </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.log&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">xargs</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">gzip</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Buscar texto em múltiplos arquivos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">find</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">.</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-name</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;*.txt&quot;</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">xargs</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">grep</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(173, 219, 103)">&quot;padrão&quot;</span><span>
</span>

</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Copiar arquivos listados</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cat</span><span> arquivos.txt </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">xargs</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-I</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">cp</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">{</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(199, 146, 234)">}</span><span> /backup/

## 103.5 - Gerenciamento de Processos

### Comando ps

**Opções principais:**

| Opção | Descrição |
|-------|-----------|
| `a` | Todos os processos com terminal |
| `u` | Formato orientado a usuário |
| `x` | Processos sem terminal |
| `e` | Todos os processos |
| `f` | Árvore de processos |
| `l` | Formato longo |
| `-p PID` | Processo específico |
| `-u user` | Processos do usuário |

**Exemplos:**
<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-8hh963x8x" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Processos do usuário atual</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ps</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Todos os processos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ps</span><span> aux
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Árvore de processos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ps</span><span> auxf
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ps</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-ejH</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Processos de usuário específico</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ps</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-u</span><span> alex
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Processo específico</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ps</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-p</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1234</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Com threads</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ps</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-eLf</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Formato customizado</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ps</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-eo</span><span> pid,user,%cpu,%mem,command
</span></code></pre></div>

**Campos importantes:**
- **USER**: Dono do processo
- **PID**: ID do processo
- **PPID**: ID do processo pai
- **%CPU**: % de CPU usado
- **%MEM**: % de memória usada
- **VSZ**: Memória virtual (KB)
- **RSS**: Memória residente (KB)
- **STAT**: Estado do processo
  - `R`: Running (executando)
  - `S`: Sleeping (dormindo)
  - `D`: Uninterruptible sleep
  - `Z`: Zombie
  - `T`: Stopped
  - `<`: Alta prioridade
  - `N`: Baixa prioridade
  - `+`: Em foreground

</span></code></pre></div>

### top - Monitor Interativo

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-uwgu9p8u7" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Iniciar top</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">top</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Processos de usuário</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">top</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-u</span><span> alex
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Atualizar a cada 2 segundos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">top</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-d</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">2</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Batch mode (não interativo)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">top</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-b</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-n</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> processos.txt
</span></code></pre></div>

**Teclas interativas no top:**
- `h` - Ajuda
- `k` - Matar processo (kill)
- `r` - Renice (mudar prioridade)
- `u` - Filtrar por usuário
- `M` - Ordenar por memória
- `P` - Ordenar por CPU
- `T` - Ordenar por tempo
- `1` - Mostrar CPUs individuais
- `q` - Sair

**Cabeçalho do top:**
```

top - 10:19:09 up 1:11, 2 users, load average: 0.02, 0.07, 0.06 Tasks: 204 total, 2 running, 202 sleeping, 0 stopped, 0 zombie %Cpu(s): 7.1 us, 0.9 sy, 0.2 ni, 91.7 id, 0.0 wa, 0.0 hi, 0.0 si, 0.0 st MiB Mem: 3530556 total, 1972072 used, 1558484 free, 232288 buffers MiB Swap: 1081340 total, 0 used, 1081340 free, 990248 cached

```

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Buscar por nome (parte do nome)</span><span>
</span>pgrep crond
<!-- -->pgrep apache
<span>pgrep </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ssh</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Com detalhes</span><span>
</span><span>pgrep </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-l</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ssh</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Por usuário</span><span>
</span><span>pgrep </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-u</span><span> alex
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Processo mais recente</span><span>
</span><span>pgrep </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-n</span><span> chrome
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Processo mais antigo</span><span>
</span><span>pgrep </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-o</span><span> systemd
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Contar processos</span><span>
</span><span>pgrep </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-c</span><span> apache
</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Nome EXATO do processo</span><span>

## Sinais de Processos

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Listar todos os sinais</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">kill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-l</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Sinais mais importantes</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

| Sinal | Número | Descrição | Uso |
|-------|--------|-----------|-----|
| SIGHUP | 1 | Hangup | Recarregar config |
| SIGINT | 2 | Interrupt | Ctrl+C |
| SIGQUIT | 3 | Quit | Ctrl+\ |
| SIGKILL | 9 | Kill | Matar forçado |
| SIGTERM | 15 | Terminate | Encerrar gentil |
| SIGCONT | 18 | Continue | Continuar processo |
| SIGSTOP | 19 | Stop | Pausar processo |

### Comando kill

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-0efr4rhi8" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Enviar SIGTERM (padrão)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">kill</span><span> PID
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">kill</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1234</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Enviar sinal específico</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">kill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-9</span><span> PID      </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># SIGKILL</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">kill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-KILL</span><span> PID   </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Mesmo que -9</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">kill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-15</span><span> PID     </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># SIGTERM</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">kill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-TERM</span><span> PID   </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Mesmo que -15</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">kill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-1</span><span> PID      </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># SIGHUP (reload)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">kill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-HUP</span><span> PID    </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Mesmo que -1</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Múltiplos processos</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">kill</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1234</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">5678</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">9012</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Recarregar configuração</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">kill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-HUP</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">$(</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">pidof apache2</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">)</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

### killall

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Matar por nome EXATO</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">killall</span><span> firefox
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">killall</span><span> apache2
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Com sinal específico</span><span>

</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">killall</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-i</span><span> firefox
</span>

### pkill

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-p2nz8iu1o" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Matar por PARTE do nome</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">pkill</span><span> chrom
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">pkill</span><span> fire
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Com sinal</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">pkill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-9</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">java</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">pkill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-HUP</span><span> rsyslog
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Por usuário</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">pkill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-u</span><span> alex
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Por terminal</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">pkill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-t</span><span> pts/0
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Processo pai específico</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">pkill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-P</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1234</span><span>
</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Encerrar todos os processos do Apache</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">killall</span><span> apache2
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Forçar encerramento do Firefox</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">pkill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-9</span><span> firefox
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Recarregar configuração do rsyslog</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">killall</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-HUP</span><span> rsyslogd
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># ou</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">kill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-HUP</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">$(</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">pidof rsyslogd</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">)</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Matar processos de usuário</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">pkill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-u</span><span> suporte
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Encerrar sessão de usuário</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">pkill</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-KILL</span><span> </span><span class="token parameter" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235)">-u</span><span> suporte
</span></code></pre></div>

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Adicionar &amp; no final</span><span>

</span><span>firefox </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&amp;</span><span>
</span>

### Controle de Jobs

<div class="widget code-container remove-before-copy"><div class="code-header non-draggable"><span class="iaf s13 w700 code-language-placeholder">bash</span><div class="code-copy-button"><span class="iaf s13 w500 code-copy-placeholder">Copiar</span><img class="code-copy-icon" src="data:image/svg+xml;utf8,%0A%3Csvg%20xmlns%3D%22http%3A%2F%2Fwww.w3.org%2F2000%2Fsvg%22%20width%3D%2216%22%20height%3D%2216%22%20viewBox%3D%220%200%2016%2016%22%20fill%3D%22none%22%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M10.8%208.63V11.57C10.8%2014.02%209.82%2015%207.37%2015H4.43C1.98%2015%201%2014.02%201%2011.57V8.63C1%206.18%201.98%205.2%204.43%205.2H7.37C9.82%205.2%2010.8%206.18%2010.8%208.63Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%20%20%3Cpath%20d%3D%22M15%204.42999V7.36999C15%209.81999%2014.02%2010.8%2011.57%2010.8H10.8V8.62999C10.8%206.17999%209.81995%205.19999%207.36995%205.19999H5.19995V4.42999C5.19995%201.97999%206.17995%200.999992%208.62995%200.999992H11.57C14.02%200.999992%2015%201.97999%2015%204.42999Z%22%20stroke%3D%22%23717C92%22%20stroke-width%3D%221.05%22%20stroke-linecap%3D%22round%22%20stroke-linejoin%3D%22round%22%2F%3E%0A%3C%2Fsvg%3E%0A" /></div></div><pre id="code-x6i5t5vxa" style="color:white;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;white-space:pre;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none;padding:8px;margin:8px;overflow:auto;background:#011627;width:calc(100% - 8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Pausar processo (Ctrl+Z)</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Processo fica stopped</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Listar jobs</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">jobs</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># [1]+ Stopped vim arquivo.txt</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Continuar em background</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">bg</span><span> %1
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">bg</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Trazer para foreground</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">fg</span><span> %1
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">fg</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">1</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Matar job</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">kill</span><span> %1
</span></code></pre></div>

**Exemplo prático:**

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Executar e manter após logout</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">nohup</span><span> comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&amp;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Saída vai para nohup.out</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">nohup</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ping</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(247, 140, 108)">8.8</span><span>.8.8 </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&amp;</span><span>
</span>

</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">nohup</span><span> comando </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span> saida.log </span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">2</span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&gt;</span><span class="token file-descriptor" style="color:rgb(214, 222, 235);font-weight:bold">&amp;1</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">&amp;</span><span>
</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Verificar</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">ps</span><span> aux </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(127, 219, 202)">|</span><span> </span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(130, 170, 255)">grep</span><span> comando

8px);border-radius:8px;box-shadow:0px 8px 18px 0px rgba(120, 120, 143, 0.10), 2px 2px 10px 0px rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.30) inset"><code class="language-bash" style="white-space:pre;color:#d6deeb;font-family:Consolas, Monaco, &quot;Andale Mono&quot;, &quot;Ubuntu Mono&quot;, monospace;text-align:left;word-spacing:normal;word-break:normal;word-wrap:normal;line-height:1.5;font-size:1em;-moz-tab-size:4;-o-tab-size:4;tab-size:4;-webkit-hyphens:none;-moz-hyphens:none;-ms-hyphens:none;hyphens:none"><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Backup que continua após logout</span><span>

</span>
<span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Logout e verificar depois</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(255, 203, 139)">exit</span><span>
</span><span></span><span class="token" style="color:rgb(99, 119, 119);font-style:italic"># Login novamente</span><span>
